∎

For the continuation of the proof of Lemma
4.6
, we use results about
short-circuit connectives and the memorising short-circuit evaluation function

\mathit{mse}(P)=\mathit{m}(\mathit{se}(P))
defined
in
[
6
]
(available online); for the function
\mathit{m}
see Definition
4.2

and below we recall the definition of the short-circuit evaluation
function
\mathit{se}
. In particular, we use the
definability of the connectives
{~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}}
and
{~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}}
in short-circuit logic
(cf. Section
7
).

Given a set of atoms
A
, the set
{\mathcal{S}_{A}}
of closed terms with short-circuit connectives
is defined by the following grammar (
a\in A
):

P::={\sf T}\mid{\sf F}\mid a\mid\neg P\mid P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}P\mid P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.54,1.54){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,1.54){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}P.

For
a\in A
, the evaluation function
\mathit{se}:{\mathcal{S}_{A}}\to\mathcal{T}_{A}
and the translation function
\mathit{t}:{\mathcal{SP}_{\!A}}\to{\mathcal{S}_{A}}

are defined by

\displaystyle\mathit{se}({\sf T})={\sf T},~\mathit{se}({\sf F})={\sf F},~~

\displaystyle\mathit{t}({\sf T})={\sf T},~~\mathit{t}({\sf F})={\sf F},

\displaystyle\mathit{se}(a)={\sf T}\unlhd a\unrhd{\sf F},

\displaystyle\mathit{t}(a)=a,~

\displaystyle\mathit{se}(\neg P)=\mathit{se}(P)[{\sf T}\mapsto{\sf F},{\sf F}\mapsto{\sf T}],~

\displaystyle\mathit{t}(\neg P)=\neg(\mathit{t}(P)),~

\displaystyle\mathit{se}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)=\mathit{se}(P)[{\sf T}\mapsto\mathit{se}(Q)],

\displaystyle\mathit{t}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)=(\mathit{t}(P)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.54,1.54){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,1.54){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}(\mathit{t}(Q)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}\mathit{t}(Q),

\displaystyle\mathit{se}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.54,1.54){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,1.54){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)=\mathit{se}(P)[{\sf F}\mapsto\mathit{se}(Q)],

\displaystyle\mathit{t}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)=(\mathit{t}(P)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}(\mathit{t}(Q)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.54,1.54){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,1.54){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf T}))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.54,1.54){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,1.54){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}\mathit{t}(Q).

Lemma B.2
.

For all
P\in{\mathcal{SP}_{\!A}}
,
\mathit{fe}(P)=\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(P))
.

Proof.

By structural induction on
P
. The base cases are immediate, and so is
\mathit{fe}(\neg P)=\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(\neg P))
.

For the case
P=Q~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}R
, derive

\displaystyle\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(P))

\displaystyle=\mathit{se}((\mathit{t}(Q)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.54,1.54){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,1.54){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}(\mathit{t}(R)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}\mathit{t}(R))

\displaystyle=\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(Q)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.54,1.54){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,1.54){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}(\mathit{t}(R)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))[{\sf T}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R))]

\displaystyle=(\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(Q))[{\sf F}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})])[{\sf T}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R))].

\displaystyle=(\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(Q))[{\sf F}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R))[{\sf T}\mapsto{\sf F}])[{\sf T}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R))]

\displaystyle=\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(Q))[{\sf T}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R)),~{\sf F}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R))[{\sf T}\mapsto{\sf F}][{\sf T}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R))]]

\displaystyle=\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(Q))[{\sf T}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R)),~{\sf F}\mapsto\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R))[{\sf T}\mapsto{\sf F}]]

\displaystyle(\ast)

\displaystyle=\mathit{fe}(Q)[{\sf T}\mapsto\mathit{fe}(R),{\sf F}\mapsto\mathit{fe}(R)[{\sf T}\mapsto{\sf F}]]

by IH

\displaystyle=\mathit{fe}(P).

(\ast)
:
By redundancy,
\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}(R))[{\sf T}\mapsto{\sf F}]
has no
{\sf T}
-leaves.

The case
P=Q~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}R
follows in a similar way.
∎

Continuation
of the proof of Lemma
4.6
.

For a variable
v\in\{x,y,z\}
, define
\mathit{t}(v)=v
. It follows with
Prover9
that

\textup{$\textrm{EqMSCL}$}\vdash\mathit{t}((x~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}y)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}z)=\mathit{t}((\neg x~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(y~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}z))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(x~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}z)),

(45)

where
EqMSCL
consists of the axioms (
Mem1
)–(
Mem5
) in Table
4
,
see Comment
B.3
below.
From
[
6
, Thm.4.1]
it follows that for all
P,Q\in{\mathcal{S}_{A}}
,

\textup{$\textrm{EqMFEL}$}\vdash P=Q\Longrightarrow\mathit{m}(\mathit{se}(P))=\mathit{m}(\mathit{se}(Q))
.
Hence, for all
P,Q,R\in{\mathcal{SP}_{\!A}}
,

\mathit{m}(\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}((P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}R)))=\mathit{m}(\mathit{se}(\mathit{t}((\neg P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(Q~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}R))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}R)))),

so, what has to be proved, i.e.,

\mathit{m}(\mathit{fe}((P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}R))=\mathit{m}(\mathit{fe}((\neg P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(Q~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}R))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}R))),

follows from Lemma
B.2
.
∎

Comment B.3
.

A proof with
Prover9
’s options
lpo
and
unfold
, and
with as assumptions the five axioms of
EqMSCL
was obtained in 0.02s.
The syntax of the proven
goal for
Prover9
, that is, the
\mathit{t}
()-translation of (
45
) where

^,v,
',1,0
stand for
{~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\wedge$}\put(-0.54,-0.2){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,-0.2){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}}},~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.54,1.54){{\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\circle*{0.6}}}\put(-0.54,1.54){\circle{0.6}}\end{picture}},\neg,{\sf T},{\sf F}
, is:

(((x ^ (y v 1)) v y) v (z ^ 0)) ^ z =
(((x’ v (((y v (z ^ 0)) ^ z) ^ 0)) ^ ((y v (z ^ 0)) ^ z)) ^ (((x v (z ^ 0)) ^ z) v 1))
v ((x v (z ^ 0)) ^ z).

Appendix C
Proofs - Section
6

We start with two auxiliary lemmas.

Lemma C.1
.

\textup{$\textrm{EqMFEL}$}\vdash(x~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(x~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}y)=(x~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(y~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}x)
.

Proof.

With
Prover9
with options
lpo
and
unfold
: 0.2s.
∎

Lemma C.2
.

For all
\beta\in A^{s}_{o}
and
P\in{\mathcal{SP}_{\!A}}
such that
\alpha(P)\subset\{\beta\}
,

\textup{$\textrm{EqMFEL}$}\vdash\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}
.

Proof.

By induction on the length of
\beta
. The base case
\beta=\epsilon
is trivial.

For
\beta=a\gamma~(a\in A)
, thus
\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}=a~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\gamma}
, we
apply structural induction on
P
, and we
note that
from Lemma
2.6
.3 and
\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\gamma}=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\gamma}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}
it follows that

\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}=(a~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\gamma}.

(C.
\dagger
)

The base cases:
1) the case

P\in\{{\sf T},{\sf F}\}

is trivial;
2) if
P=a
, then by (
C.

†

) and the dual of
(
C1
)
,
\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(a~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})=((a~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\gamma})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(a~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}
;
3) if
P\in\{\gamma\}
, then by (
C.

†

),

\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})=((a~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\gamma})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})=(a~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\gamma}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{IH}}}{{=}}(a~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\gamma}=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}
.

If
P=\neg P_{1}
, then
\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(\neg P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{FFEL8}}}{{=}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{IH}}}{{=}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}
.

If
P=P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P_{2}
, then
\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}((P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P_{2})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}((P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P_{2}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{C4}^{\prime}}}{{=}}\\
(\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P_{2}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{IH}}}{{=}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}
, where
\eqref{C4}^{\prime}
is the dual of
(
C4
).

If
P=P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P_{2}
, then
\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}((P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P_{2})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{M1}}}{{=}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}((\neg P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P_{2}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{La.\ref{la:feqs}.1}}}{{=}}\\
\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}((P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P_{2}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))=(\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}((P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P_{2})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(pc),\text{IH}}}{{=}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}
,

where (pc) refers to the previous case
P=P_{1}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P_{2}
.
∎

Continuation
of the proof of Lemma
6.7
.

1. Let
P,Q\in{\mathcal{SP}_{\!A}}
. We have to prove that

\mathit{mfe}(\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q))=\mathit{mfe}(\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(Q~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P)),

where
\beta\in A^{s}_{o}
satisfies
\{\beta\}=\alpha(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)
.
By soundness of
EqMFEL
(Lemma
4.6
), it suffices to prove
that for any
\beta\in A^{s}_{o}
that satisfies
\alpha(P)\subset\{\beta\}
,

\textup{$\textrm{EqMFEL}$}\vdash\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(Q~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P).

Derive

\displaystyle\textup{$\textrm{EqMFEL}$}\vdash\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)

\displaystyle=(\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F}))~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)

by Lemma
C.2

\displaystyle=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}((P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q))

\displaystyle=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}((P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}{\sf F})~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(Q~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P))

by Lemma
C.1

\displaystyle=\widetilde{}{\sf F}_{\beta}~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.0){$\vee$}\put(-0.42,1.4){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}(Q~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P).

(as above)

2. The soundness of
\textup{$\textrm{EqC$\ell$FEL}$}^{\sf U}
follows as in 1, the additional case is that
for any
P\in{\mathcal{SP}_{\!A}}^{\!{\sf U}}\setminus{\mathcal{SP}_{\!A}}
and
Q\in{\mathcal{SP}_{\!A}}^{\!{\sf U}}
,

\mathit{clfe}(P~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}Q)=\mathit{clfe}(Q~\mathbin{\begin{picture}(-0.3,0.0)\put(-0.6,0.2){$\wedge$}\put(-0.46,-0.0){\circle*{0.6}}\end{picture}}P)={\sf U}.

∎
